# Group debts, profile persistence, and accessibility

**Session ID:** ses_e0ca567acffeS4aalCd4WhXdhA
**Created:** 11/19/2026, 11:29:23 AM
**Updated:** 11/19/2026, 12:44:26 PM

---

## User

Do so every user in a group can create a debt, and not just the owner of the group, also no user, neither the owner, should be able to create a debt "for" another user.

Mails not found in the system, should not be addable to a group.

Also it's not actively saving changes to a profile to the database, because if I change my name, it does save throughout the active session in the app, but it doesn't change for other users, and when I relogin, its back to the data that I created the account with. It should save.

At D:\Downloads\Icons.zip, you will find a bunch of icons for the application, below is a list of which icons should be used where.

add: create group button
arrow_back: back button
cancel: failed payment
check_circle: payment success
credit_card: payment methods
download: export receipt
edit: edit profile
error: error message
group: groups tab
logout: log out button
notifications: notifications bell
notifications_active: remind button
payments: make payment button
person: profile tab
person_add: add participant
person_remove: remove participant
receipt: payment history item
schedule: pending payment
search: search field
settings: settings tab 

And below is our analysis for which Typography to use in the application as well, implement it too.

ISO 9241-303:2011 5.4.5 covers why rows and columns strength matters based on the width of a character, this continues onto 5.5.X
ISO 9241-303:2011 5.5.1 and 5.5.2 to 5.5.4 covers why letters matter and why it should be covered
5.5.2 Luminance contrast based on elderly people in mind
5.5.3 Image polarity (Negative and positive image polarity) based on elderly people in mind "This can matter in terms of correct background color, also refer to ISO 9241-125 4.X.X for this"
5.5.4 Character height and why it matters on certain letter origins, here focus being Latin letters with elders in mind, Refer to ISO 9241-125 4.X.X and 5.X.X for visual classification
Google Docs font sizes are specified in typographic points (pt). To compare these values with ISO 9241-303 recommendations, the nominal point size must be translated into actual character height and evaluated relative to viewing distance, since ISO legibility requirements are based on visual angle rather than point size alone.
Sans-Serif
Typographic mechanics: Lacks horizontal guiding strokes, which can increase tracking effort in dense, continuous blocks of text like textbooks or long reports.
Reader perception & UI suitability: Readers generally prefer sans-serif on screens because the clean, uncluttered letterforms reduce visual noise, making it ideal for applications and non-cramped layouts, where users need to maintain focus and scan data quickly.
Serif
Typographic mechanics: The small terminal strokes (serifs) create an invisible horizontal baseline that guides the eye, allowing more text to be packed into a reading area without the reader losing their place across lines.
Reader perception & UI suitability: In digital interfaces, those extra strokes add visual complexity that can feel fatiguing over long periods, making readers less inclined to prefer them for screen-based tasks.
Deciding on the typography for the application, Sans- text would be ideal. As there’s little to no text to read from that moves around necessarily, making it less likely for the reader to lose track of what line they’re specifically at; the application will also for the most part index and table most text and values, making it easier to re-track onto where they left reading.
Preferred text based on analysis:

Positive image polarity
Negativ image polarity
Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet, consectetur adipiscing elit, sed do eiusmod tempor incididunt ut labore et dolore magna aliqua.
Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet, consectetur adipiscing elit, sed do eiusmod tempor incididunt ut labore et dolore magna aliqua.

Note: 12 pt is approximately 16 px height with 1.516px =24px line spacing
The analysis does not account for reading angle and viewing distance as specified in the ISO standards, so minimum standards are followed instead.
List format for required typography:
Typography type: Sans-Serif of any type
Only use of structured latin letters is allowed “no Caveat ”
16 px height minimum “12 pt”
24 px line height minimum “1.516px =24px”
Required clear negative image polarity if added “text MUST be white”
Required clear positive image polarity if added “text MUST be black”

39:         amountCents: Long,
40:         description: String,
41:         debtId: String? = null,
42:     ) = viewModelScope.launch(io) {
43:         _saveResult.value = RequestStatus.Pending
44:         _saveResult.value = runRequest {
45:             require(amountCents > 0L) { "Enter an amount greater than 0." }
46:             require(payerId.isNotBlank()) { "Not logged in." }
47:             if (debtId == null) {
48:                 val members = (groups.observeGroup(groupId).first { it is RequestStatus.Success }
49:                     as RequestStatus.Success).data?.memberIds?.ifEmpty { null }
50:                     ?: listOf(payerId)
51:                 val expense = Expense(
52:                     id = "",
53:                     groupId = groupId,
54:                     payerId = payerId,
55:                     amountCents = amountCents,
56:                     description = description.ifBlank { "Debt" },
57:                     splitAmong = members,
58:                 )
59:                 when (val r = expenses.addExpense(expense)) {
60:                     is RequestStatus.Success -> r.data
61:                     is RequestStatus.Failed -> throw IllegalStateException(r.message ?: "Save failed.")
62:                     is RequestStatus.Pending -> throw IllegalStateException("Save pending.")
63:                 }
64:             } else {
65:                 // Blank note keeps the stored text (null = "no change" on this contract).
66:                 when (val r = expenses.updateExpense(
67:                     debtId,
68:                     amountCents = amountCents,
69:                     description = description.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() },
70:                 )) {
71:                     is RequestStatus.Success -> r.data
72:                     is RequestStatus.Failed -> throw IllegalStateException(r.message ?: "Save failed.")
73:                     is RequestStatus.Pending -> throw IllegalStateException("Save pending.")
74:                 }
75:             }
76:         }
77:     }
78: 
79:     /** Existing expense for the edit route (null data = unknown id); drives field preload. */
80:     fun editingExpense(groupId: String, debtId: String): Flow<RequestStatus<Expense?>> =
81:         expenses.observeExpenses(groupId).map { status ->
82:             when (status) {
83:                 is RequestStatus.Success -> RequestStatus.Success(status.data.firstOrNull { it.id == debtId })
84:                 is RequestStatus.Pending -> RequestStatus.Pending
85:                 is RequestStatus.Failed -> RequestStatus.Failed(status.message, status.retryable)
86:             }
87:         }

90:         _saveResult.value = null
91:     }

23: /**
24:  * Set up debt. Persisting runs in the VM owner ([onSave] stores an expense split among
25:  * the group members); [saveResult] success fires [onDone] (debtId != null = edit existing).
26:  *
27:  * [participants] are real group members (id + display name) from GroupDetailViewModel;
28:  * only member ids are ever passed to [onSave] — never display names. Empty list means
29:  * the group hasn't loaded: Save stays disabled and an error shows instead of writing
30:  * an invalid payer id. Edit mode ([debtId] != null) prefills [initialAmountCents] /
31:  * [initialNote] when the owner passes them.
32:  *
33:  * Pending shows progress with the form disabled; failed shows the repo message with
34:  * Retry (re-submits the last values) and Dismiss ([onDismissError] clears the VM
35:  * result). Back stays available throughout — failure never traps the user.
36:  */
37: @Composable
38: fun SetupDebtScreen(
39:     modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
40:     groupId: String,
41:     debtId: String? = null,
42:     onDone: () -> Unit,
43:     saveResult: RequestStatus<Expense>? = null,
44:     onSave: (String, Long, String) -> Unit = { _, _, _ -> },
45:     groupName: String? = null,
46:     participants: List<MemberUi> = emptyList(),
47:     initialPayerId: String? = null,
48:     initialAmountCents: Long? = null,
49:     initialNote: String = "",
50:     onDismissError: () -> Unit = {},
51: ) {
52:     var amount by remember(debtId) {
53:         mutableStateOf(initialAmountCents?.let { "%.2f".format(it / 100.0) } ?: "")
54:     }
55:     var note by remember(debtId) { mutableStateOf(initialNote) }
56:     var payerId by remember(participants, initialPayerId) {
57:         mutableStateOf(
58:             initialPayerId?.takeIf { id -> participants.any { it.id == id } }
59:                 ?: participants.firstOrNull()?.id.orEmpty(),
60:         )
61:     }
62:     var lastSubmitted by remember { mutableStateOf<Triple<String, Long, String>?>(null) }
63:     val cents = amount.toDoubleOrNull()?.let { (it * 100).roundToLong() }
64:     val valid = cents != null && cents > 0L
65:     val isSaving = saveResult is RequestStatus.Pending
66:     val isFailed = saveResult is RequestStatus.Failed
67:     val byId = remember(participants) { participants.associateBy { it.id } }
68:     val payerValid = payerId.isNotBlank() && byId.containsKey(payerId)
69:     val membersMissing = participants.isEmpty()
70:     LaunchedEffect(saveResult) {
71:         if (saveResult is RequestStatus.Success) onDone()
72:     }
73:     ScreenScaffold(
74:         title = if (debtId == null) "Set up debt" else "Edit debt",
75:         modifier = modifier,
76:         onBack = onDone,
77:     ) {
78:         Text("Group: ${groupName ?: "Loading…"}")
79:         MemberDropdown(
80:             label = "Paid by",
81:             valueId = payerId,
82:             options = participants,
83:             enabled = !isSaving && !membersMissing,
84:             onSelect = { payerId = it },
85:         )
86:         OutlinedTextField(
87:             value = amount,
88:             onValueChange = { amount = it },
89:             label = { Text("Amount") },
90:             singleLine = true,
91:             enabled = !isSaving,
92:             keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Decimal),
93:             modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
94:         )
95:         OutlinedTextField(
96:             value = note,
97:             onValueChange = { note = it },
98:             label = { Text("Note") },
99:             singleLine = true,
100:             enabled = !isSaving,
101:             modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
102:         )
103:         if (membersMissing) {
104:             Text(
105:                 "Group members unavailable — cannot save a debt yet.",
106:                 color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
107:             )
108:         }
109:         RequestStatusBanner(
110:             status = saveResult,
111:             pendingLabel = "Saving debt…",
112:             onRetry = if (isFailed) {
113:                 { lastSubmitted?.let { onSave(it.first, it.second, it.third) } }
114:             } else {
115:                 null
116:             },
117:             onDismiss = if (isFailed) onDismissError else null,
118:         )
119:         NavButton(
120:             if (isSaving) "Saving…" else "Save debt",
121:             enabled = valid && payerValid && !isSaving,
122:             onClick = {
123:                 lastSubmitted = Triple(payerId, cents!!, note)
124:                 onSave(payerId, cents, note)
125:             },
126:         )
127:     }
128: }
129:

50: /**

55: internal fun detailFirstLoad(

57:     summary: RequestStatus<DebtSummary>,
58:     refreshedOnce: Boolean,
59: ): Boolean = group is RequestStatus.Pending || summary is RequestStatus.Pending ||
60:     (!refreshedOnce && (
61:         (group is RequestStatus.Success && group.data == null) ||
62:             (summary is RequestStatus.Success && summary.data.settlements.isEmpty())

70: @HiltViewModel

87:     /** Last explicit-refresh failure (null when fresh); the nav error slot reads this. */

89:     val refreshError: StateFlow<String?> = _refreshError.asStateFlow()
90: 
91:     init {
92:         launchRefresh()
93:     }
94: 
95:     /** Both refreshes run even if one fails; refreshedOnce lands only when both succeed. */
96:     private fun launchRefresh() = viewModelScope.launch(io) {
97:         // Boolean contract: false = network failed (cached data still shows).
98:         val ok = supervisorScope {
99:             listOf(
100:                 async { runCatching { groups.refreshGroup(groupId) }.getOrDefault(false) },
101:                 async { runCatching { expenses.refreshExpenses(groupId) }.getOrDefault(false) },
102:             ).awaitAll().all { it }
103:         }
104:         if (ok) {
105:             _refreshedOnce.value = true
106:             _refreshError.value = null
107:         } else {
108:             _refreshError.value = "Couldn't refresh group data."

117:     // TODO(api-integration): prefer GET /api/groups/:id/summary when online; keep this pure

122:     val summary: StateFlow<RequestStatus<DebtSummary>> = expensesStatus
123:         .map { status ->
124:             when (status) {
125:                 is RequestStatus.Success -> RequestStatus.Success(DebtCalculator.summarize(status.data))
126:                 is RequestStatus.Pending -> RequestStatus.Pending
127:                 is RequestStatus.Failed -> RequestStatus.Failed(status.message, status.retryable)
128:             }
129:         }
130:         .flowOn(io)
131:         .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5_000), RequestStatus.Pending)

133:     /** Missing group / empty summary counts as loading until the first refresh lands. */

197:     /** Error-state retry: clears the refresh error, re-subscribes + runs one fresh pass. */
198:     fun retry() {
199:         retryTick.value++
200:         _refreshError.value = null
201:         launchRefresh()
202:     }

207:      * the repo updates the cached group, so [group]/[members] re-emit on their own.

211: 
212:     fun removeMember(userId: String) =
213:         viewModelScope.launch(io) {
214:             _removeResult.value = RequestStatus.Pending
215:             _removeResult.value = runRequest {
216:                 require(userId.isNotBlank()) { "Select a member to remove." }
217:                 when (val r = groups.removeMember(groupId, userId)) {
218:                     is RequestStatus.Success -> Unit
219:                     is RequestStatus.Failed -> throw IllegalStateException(r.message ?: "Remove failed.")
220:                     is RequestStatus.Pending -> throw IllegalStateException("Remove pending.")
221:                 }
222:             }
223:         }
224:

226:         _removeResult.value = null
227:     }

240:             _inviteResult.value = runRequest {
241:                 val trimmed = email.trim()
242:                 require(trimmed.isNotBlank() && trimmed.contains("@")) { "Enter a valid email address." }
243:                 when (val r = groups.addMember(groupId, trimmed)) {
244:                     is RequestStatus.Success -> Unit
245:                     is RequestStatus.Failed -> throw IllegalStateException(r.message ?: "Invite failed.")
246:                     is RequestStatus.Pending -> throw IllegalStateException("Invite pending.")
247:                 }

260:     private val _saveResult = MutableStateFlow<RequestStatus<Group>?>(null)

262:

265:             _saveResult.value = RequestStatus.Pending
266:             _saveResult.value = runRequest {
267:                 require(name.isNotBlank()) { "Group name must not be blank." }
268:                 when (val r = groups.updateGroup(groupId, name.trim(), null)) {
269:                     is RequestStatus.Success -> r.data

272:                 }

274:         }
275:

281: /** Self → cached name ("You" when blank) → contact email → email-like id → short ref. */
282: private fun displayName(id: UserId, me: User?, cached: User?): String {
283:     if (id == me?.id) return me.name.ifBlank { "You" }
284:     if (!cached?.name.isNullOrBlank()) return cached.name
285:     // Repo-upserted User(name="", contact=email) rows render the full email here;
286:     // pending email-keyed invites surface as email-like ids and hit the same branch.
287:     // Never a raw UUID: worst case is the shared memberShortRef (same as displayNameOrRef).
288:     val email = cached?.contact?.takeIf { it.contains("@") } ?: id.takeIf { it.contains("@") }
289:     if (email != null) return email
290:     return memberShortRef(id)
291: }
292: 
293: /** Domain entry → screen row; txRef pre-shortens the payment id (== txId, no backend tx field). */
294: private fun GroupHistoryEntry.toUi(nameById: Map<UserId, String>): GroupHistoryUi = GroupHistoryUi(
295:     id = id,
296:     kind = if (kind == GroupHistoryKind.PAYMENT) HistoryKind.PAYMENT else HistoryKind.DEBT,
297:     title = title,
298:     payerName = displayNameOrRef(nameById, payerId),
299:     ownerName = displayNameOrRef(nameById, counterpartyId),
300:     amountCents = amountCents,
301:     txRef = if (kind == GroupHistoryKind.PAYMENT) shortIdRef(id) else null,
302: )

568: /**
569:  * Auth-gate: fires [onUnauthenticated] exactly when the session VM reports logged-out.
570:  * Pending (token read in flight) renders nothing — no flash, no graph hack.
571:  */
572: @Composable
573: private fun RequireAuth(session: RequestStatus<User?>, onUnauthenticated: () -> Unit) {
574:     if (session is RequestStatus.Success && session.data == null) {
575:         LaunchedEffect(Unit) { onUnauthenticated() }
576:     }
577: }
578: 
579: /**
580:  * Sign-out: awaits the session + cache clear via [AuthViewModel] before landing on Login
581:  * (popped, so Back never returns to authenticated screens), closing the race where a
582:  * stale session re-triggered the Login self-heal or a refresh repopulated Room after
583:  * `clearAllTables`. Mirrors the RequireAuth redirect (same popUpTo); launchSingleTop
584:  * dedupes against its auto-redirect.
585:  */
586: private suspend fun signOut(authVm: AuthViewModel, navController: NavHostController) {
587:     authVm.signOut()
588:     navController.navigate(Routes.Login) {
589:         popUpTo(navController.graph.findStartDestination().id) { inclusive = true }
590:         launchSingleTop = true
591:     }
592: }
593: 
594: /** Fallback group ref for ids missing from the cached groups — never a full UUID. */
595: private fun shortGroupRef(id: String): String =
596:     if (id.length > 8) "Group ••••${id.takeLast(4)}" else id
597: 
598: /** Already-authed users never sit on Login/Register (covers the slow-token-read race). */@Composable
599: private fun ForwardWhenAuthenticated(session: RequestStatus<User?>, navController: NavHostController) {
600:     if ((session as? RequestStatus.Success)?.data != null) {
601:         LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
602:             navController.navigate(Routes.groups()) {
603:                 popUpTo(Routes.Login) { inclusive = true }
604:             }
605:         }
606:     }
607: }
608: 
609: @Composable
610: private fun BottomTabs(selected: String?, onTab: (String) -> Unit) {
611:     // Text glyphs: no icons dep in this slice.
612:     val tabs = listOf(
613:         "Groups" to Routes.groups(),
614:         "Account" to Routes.Account,
615:         "Settings" to Routes.Settings,
616:     )
617:     NavigationBar {
618:         tabs.forEach { (label, route) ->
619:             val isSelected = selected == route || (label == "Groups" && selected == Routes.Groups)
620:             NavigationBarItem(
621:                 selected = isSelected,
622:                 onClick = { if (!isSelected) onTab(route) },
623:                 icon = { Text(label.take(1)) },
624:                 label = { Text(label) },
625:             )
626:         }
627:     }
628: }

27: /**
28:  * UserRepository: member cache reads come from Room (backend has no user-list
29:  * route, so [upsertUser] is local-only); the device profile is DataStore-backed
30:  * (source of truth, survives restart) with Room as mirror. No PATCH /me route
31:  * exists, so /me sync is explicit via [refreshMe] and never clobbers local
32:  * name/contact. Auth extras ([register]/[login]/[signOut])
33:  * persist the JWT via [UserPreferences]. 401 clears the token.
34:  */
35: @Singleton
36: class ApiUserRepository @Inject constructor(
37:     private val api: ApiService,
38:     private val db: EasyTrackerDatabase,
39:     private val prefs: UserPreferences,
40:     @IoDispatcher private val io: CoroutineDispatcher,
41: ) : UserRepository {
42:     private val users = db.userDao()
43:     private val clear401: suspend () -> Unit = { prefs.clearAuth() }
44: 
45:     override fun observeUsers(): Flow<RequestStatus<List<User>>> =
46:         users.observeAll().map { rows ->
47:             RequestStatus.Success(rows.map { it.toDomain() }.sortedBy(User::name)) as RequestStatus<List<User>>
48:         }.onStart { emit(RequestStatus.Pending) }.flowOn(io)
49: 
50:     override fun observeUser(userId: UserId): Flow<RequestStatus<User?>> =
51:         users.observeById(userId).map { row ->
52:             RequestStatus.Success(row?.toDomain()) as RequestStatus<User?>
53:         }.onStart { emit(RequestStatus.Pending) }.flowOn(io)
54: 
55:     override suspend fun upsertUser(user: User): RequestStatus<User> = withContext(io) {
56:         if (user.name.isBlank()) return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("User name must not be blank")
57:         users.upsert(user.toEntity())
58:         RequestStatus.Success(user)
59:     }
60: 
61:     override suspend fun updateNotificationPrefs(userId: UserId, value: NotificationPrefs): RequestStatus<Unit> =
62:         withContext(io) {
63:             val current = users.observeById(userId).first() ?: return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("User not found")
64:             users.upsert(current.toDomain().copy(notificationPrefs = value).toEntity())
65:             if (prefs.profile.first()?.id == userId) prefs.saveNotificationPrefs(value)
66:             RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
67:         }
68: 
69:     override fun observeCurrentProfile(): Flow<RequestStatus<User?>> =
70:         prefs.profile.map { RequestStatus.Success(it) as RequestStatus<User?> }
71:             .onStart { emit(RequestStatus.Pending) }.flowOn(io)
72: 
73:     /** Local-only save (no PATCH /me route): DataStore first, Room mirror second. */
74:     override suspend fun saveCurrentProfile(user: User): RequestStatus<Unit> = withContext(io) {
75:         if (user.name.isBlank()) return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("Name must not be blank.")
76:         prefs.saveProfile(user)
77:         users.upsert(user.toEntity())
78:         RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
79:     }
80: 
81:     /** POST /auth/register — stores token + profile on success. */
82:     suspend fun register(name: String, email: String, password: String): RequestStatus<User> = withContext(io) {
83:         when (val r = safeApiCall { api.register(RegisterRequest(name.trim(), email.trim(), password)) }) {
84:             is RequestStatus.Success -> persistAuth(r.data.token, r.data.user.toDomain())
85:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
86:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
87:         }
88:     }
89: 
90:     /** POST /auth/login — stores token + profile on success. */
91:     suspend fun login(email: String, password: String): RequestStatus<User> = withContext(io) {
92:         when (val r = safeApiCall { api.login(LoginRequest(email.trim(), password)) }) {
93:             is RequestStatus.Success -> persistAuth(r.data.token, r.data.user.toDomain())
94:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
95:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
96:         }
97:     }
98: 
99:     /**
100:      * GET /me — explicit best-effort sync (never auto-run from [observeCurrentProfile]).
101:      * No PATCH /me route exists, so DataStore stays source of truth for name/contact:
102:      * a local profile keeps its name/contact and only the Room mirror is refreshed.
103:      */
104:     suspend fun refreshMe(): RequestStatus<User> = withContext(io) {
105:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.me() }) {
106:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
107:                 // Signed out while in flight: never rewrite the profile after the wipe.
108:                 if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) {
109:                     return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("Signed out", retryable = false)
110:                 }
111:                 val keep = prefs.profile.first()
112:                 val user = keep?.copy(id = r.data.id) ?: r.data.toDomain()
113:                 prefs.saveProfile(user)
114:                 users.upsert(user.toEntity())
115:                 RequestStatus.Success(user)
116:             }
117:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
118:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
119:         }
120:     }
121: 
122:     /** Sign-out: clears DataStore session + profile and wipes the Room cache. */
123:     suspend fun signOut(): Unit = withContext(io) {
124:         prefs.clear()
125:         db.clearAllTables()
126:     }
127: 
128:     private suspend fun persistAuth(token: String, user: User): RequestStatus<User> {
129:         // toDomain folds the backend email into contact, so contact carries the email here.
130:         prefs.saveAuth(token, user.id, user.contact)
131:         val keep = prefs.profile.first()?.notificationPrefs ?: NotificationPrefs()
132:         val merged = user.copy(notificationPrefs = keep)
133:         prefs.saveProfile(merged)
134:         users.upsert(merged.toEntity())
135:         return RequestStatus.Success(merged)
136:     }
137: }

66: fun GroupDetailScreen(
67:     modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
68:     groupId: String,

72:     onSetupDebt: (String) -> Unit,

75:     members: List<MemberUi> = emptyList(),

84:     debtProgress: Map<String, PaidProgress> = emptyMap(),
85:     historyIsLoading: Boolean = false,
86:     historyError: String? = null,
87:     historyHasLoaded: Boolean = true,
88: ) {

120: @Composable
121: private fun GroupDetailContent(
122:     groupId: String,
123:     members: List<MemberUi>,
124:     debts: List<String>,
125:     isOwner: Boolean,
126:     onMakePayment: (String) -> Unit,
127:     onEditGroup: (String) -> Unit,
128:     onSetupDebt: (String) -> Unit,
129:     removeResult: RequestStatus<Unit>?,
130:     onRemoveMember: (String) -> Unit,
131:     onDismissRemoveResult: () -> Unit,
132:     history: List<GroupHistoryUi> = emptyList(),
133:     debtProgress: Map<String, PaidProgress> = emptyMap(),
134:     historyIsLoading: Boolean = false,
135:     historyError: String? = null,
136:     historyHasLoaded: Boolean = true,
137:     onHistoryRetry: () -> Unit = {},
138:     modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
139: ) {
140:     var pendingRemove: MemberUi? by remember { mutableStateOf(null) }
141:     val removing = removeResult is RequestStatus.Pending
142:     Column(modifier = modifier, verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)) {
143:         // Members card: owners see status chips + invite/remove; members see plain rows.
144:         SectionCard(title = "Members", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
145:             if (members.isEmpty()) {
146:                 Text(
147:                     "No members yet.",
148:                     style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
149:                     color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
150:                 )
151:             } else {
152:                 members.forEach { member ->
153:                     Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
154:                         MemberChip(
155:                             name = member.name,
156:                             status = if (isOwner) member.status else null,
157:                             modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(vertical = 4.dp),
158:                         )
159:                         if (isOwner) {
160:                             TextButton(
161:                                 onClick = { pendingRemove = member },
162:                                 enabled = !removing,
163:                                 modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = 48.dp)
164:                                     .semantics { contentDescription = "Remove ${member.name}" },
165:                             ) { Text(if (removing) "…" else "Remove") }
166:                         }
167:                     }
168:                 }
169:             }
170:             // Repo message verbatim (403 owner-only etc); dismiss clears the VM state.
171:             if (removeResult is RequestStatus.Failed) {
172:                 Text(
173:                     removeResult.message ?: "Couldn't remove that member.",
174:                     style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
175:                     color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
176:                 )
177:                 TextButton(
178:                     onClick = onDismissRemoveResult,
179:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = 48.dp),
180:                 ) { Text("Dismiss") }
181:             }
182:             if (isOwner) {
183:                 OutlinedButton(
184:                     onClick = { onEditGroup(groupId) },
185:                     modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(min = 48.dp),
186:                 ) { Text("Add member") }
187:             }
188:         }
189:         // Overview card: totals + actions.
190:         SectionCard(title = "Overview", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
191:             if (debts.isEmpty()) {
192:                 EmptyState(
193:                     title = "No debts yet",
194:                     message = "All settled up — set up a debt to get started.",
195:                 )
196:             } else {
197:                 Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
198:                     debts.forEach { debt ->
199:                         Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
200:                             Text(debt, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge)
201:                             debtProgress[debt]?.let { progress ->
202:                                 Text(
203:                                     "Paid ${euros(progress.paidCents)} of ${euros(progress.totalCents)}",
204:                                     style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
205:                                     color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
206:                                 )
207:                                 if (progress.totalCents > 0) {
208:                                     LinearProgressIndicator(
209:                                         progress = {
210:                                             (progress.paidCents.toFloat() / progress.totalCents.toFloat())
211:                                                 .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
212:                                         },
213:                                         modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
214:                                     )
215:                                 }
216:                             }
217:                         }
218:                     }
219:                 }
220:             }
221:             FilledTonalButton(
222:                 onClick = { onMakePayment(groupId) },
223:                 modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(min = 48.dp),
224:             ) { Text("Make payment") }
225:             if (isOwner) {
226:                 SecondaryButton("Edit group", onClick = { onEditGroup(groupId) })
227:                 SecondaryButton("Set up a debt", onClick = { onSetupDebt(groupId) })
228:             }
229:         }
230:         // History card: per-group payments + debts for owner/payer tracking.
231:         SectionCard(title = "History", modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
232:             when {
233:                 historyIsLoading || (!historyHasLoaded && historyError == null && history.isEmpty()) ->
234:                     LoadingState("Loading history…")
235:                 history.isEmpty() && historyError != null ->
236:                     ErrorState(historyError, onRetry = onHistoryRetry)
237:                 history.isEmpty() -> EmptyState(
238:                     title = "No activity yet",
239:                     message = "Payments and debts in this group will show up here.",
240:                 )
241:                 else -> Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
242:                     history.forEach { entry ->
243:                         Column(
244:                             verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp),
245:                             modifier = Modifier.semantics {
246:                                 contentDescription = "${entry.title} ${euros(entry.amountCents)}"
247:                             },
248:                         ) {
249:                             Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
250:                                 Text(
251:                                     text = entry.title.ifBlank {
252:                                         if (entry.kind == HistoryKind.DEBT) "Debt" else "Payment"
253:                                     },
254:                                     style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
255:                                     modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
256:                                 )
257:                                 AmountText(entry.amountCents)
258:                             }
259:                             Text(
260:                                 text = historySubtitle(entry),
261:                                 style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
262:                                 color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
263:                             )
264:                         }
265:                     }
266:                 }
267:             }
268:         }
269:     }
270:     // Confirm every removal — including self — before hitting the repo.
271:     pendingRemove?.let { target ->
272:         AlertDialog(
273:             onDismissRequest = { pendingRemove = null },
274:             title = { Text("Remove ${target.name}?") },
275:             text = { Text("${target.name} will lose access to this group.") },
276:             confirmButton = {
277:                 TextButton(
278:                     onClick = { onRemoveMember(target.id); pendingRemove = null },
279:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = 48.dp),
280:                 ) { Text("Remove") }
281:             },
282:             dismissButton = {
283:                 TextButton(
284:                     onClick = { pendingRemove = null },
285:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = 48.dp),
286:                 ) { Text("Cancel") }
287:             },
288:         )
289:     }
290: }

326: /** Never a full id — display names win, ids collapse to ••••last4. */
327: private fun shortRef(id: String): String =
328:     if (id.length > 8) "••••${id.takeLast(4)}" else id.ifBlank { "••••????" }
329: 
330: private fun euros(cents: Long): String = "€${"%.2f".format(cents / 100.0)}"
331: 
332: private fun historySubtitle(entry: GroupHistoryUi): String {
333:     val payer = entry.payerName ?: "Someone"
334:     val owner = entry.ownerName
335:     val parties = if (owner != null) "$payer → $owner" else payer
336:     val tag = if (entry.kind == HistoryKind.DEBT) "Debt" else "Paid"
337:     return "$tag • $parties"
338: }

36: /**
37:  * Offline-first GroupRepository: Room cache is the source of truth for reads
38:  * (stays visible offline); every read triggers a best-effort network refresh,
39:  * writes go to the network first then update the cache. 401 clears the token.
40:  * Fake* repos stay for tests. No Hilt binding yet — arch owner wires it.
41:  */
42: @Singleton
43: class ApiGroupRepository @Inject constructor(
44:     private val api: ApiService,
45:     db: EasyTrackerDatabase,
46:     private val prefs: UserPreferences,
47:     @IoDispatcher private val io: CoroutineDispatcher,
48: ) : GroupRepository {
49:     private val groups = db.groupDao()
50:     private val users = db.userDao()
51:     private val clear401: suspend () -> Unit = { prefs.clearAuth() }
52:     // Singleton-lived: background refreshes never block collectors (cache emits first).
53:     private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + io)
54:     private val listRefresh = RefreshSingleFlight()
55:     private val itemRefresh = KeyedRefreshSingleFlight()
56:     // Last network failure per pass; an empty cache + a recorded failure emits Failed
57:     // (a warm cache always wins, so offline reads stay visible).
58:     private val listError = MutableStateFlow<RequestStatus.Failed?>(null)
59:     private val itemErrors = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, RequestStatus.Failed>>(emptyMap())
60: 
61:     // Cache-first: Room emits immediately, network refreshes in the background single-flighted,
62:     // so N collectors/recompositions share one pass and never gate the first frame on RTT.
63:     // Empty cache + failed refresh surfaces Failed (retryable) instead of a fake empty Success.
64:     override fun observeGroups(): Flow<RequestStatus<List<Group>>> =
65:         combine(groups.observeAll(), listError) { rows, err ->
66:             val list = rows.map { it.toDomain() }.sortedBy(Group::name)
67:             if (list.isEmpty() && err != null) err else RequestStatus.Success(list)
68:         }.onStart {
69:             listRefresh.launchIn(scope) { fetchGroups() }
70:         }.flowOn(io)
71: 
72:     override fun observeGroup(groupId: GroupId): Flow<RequestStatus<Group?>> =
73:         combine(groups.observeById(groupId), itemErrors) { row, errs ->
74:             val group = row?.toDomain()
75:             val err = errs[groupId]
76:             if (group == null && err != null) err else RequestStatus.Success(group)
77:         }.onStart {
78:             itemRefresh.launchIn(scope, groupId) { fetchGroup(groupId) }
79:         }.flowOn(io)
80: 
81:     override suspend fun createGroup(
82:         name: String,
83:         description: String,
84:         ownerId: UserId,
85:         memberIds: List<UserId>,
86:     ): RequestStatus<Group> = withContext(io) {
87:         if (name.isBlank()) return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("Group name must not be blank")
88:         val emails = memberIds.filter { it != ownerId }.mapNotNull { emailOf(it) }
89:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) {
90:             api.createGroup(CreateGroupRequest(name.trim(), description, emails))
91:         }) {
92:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
93:                 val group = r.data.toDomain()
94:                 groups.upsert(group.toEntity())
95:                 cacheMembers(r.data)
96:                 RequestStatus.Success(group)
97:             }
98:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
99:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
100:         }
101:     }
102: 
103:     override suspend fun addMember(groupId: GroupId, userId: UserId): RequestStatus<Unit> = withContext(io) {
104:         val email = emailOf(userId) ?: return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("No email for member")
105:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.addMember(groupId, AddMemberRequest(email)) }) {
106:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
107:                 // Direct-add contract: POST returns updated group with new ACCEPTED
108:                 // member; cache it immediately so inviter sees member without refetch.
109:                 // dto.members carries the new member's name/email, so the User row
110:                 // lands too — displayName() resolves instantly.
111:                 groups.upsert(r.data.toDomain().toEntity())
112:                 cacheMembers(r.data)
113:                 RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
114:             }
115:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> if (runCatching { refreshGroup(groupId) }.getOrDefault(false)) {
116:                 RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
117:             } else {
118:                 r
119:             }
120:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
121:         }
122:     }
123: 
124:     override suspend fun removeMember(groupId: GroupId, userId: UserId): RequestStatus<Unit> = withContext(io) {
125:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.removeMember(groupId, userId) }) {
126:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
127:                 runCatching { refreshGroup(groupId) }
128:                 RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
129:             }
130:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
131:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
132:         }
133:     }
134: 
135:     override suspend fun respondToInvite(groupId: GroupId, userId: UserId, accept: Boolean): RequestStatus<Unit> =
136:         withContext(io) {
137:             // userId is the local caller; backend derives the invitee from the JWT.
138:             when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.respondToInvite(groupId, InviteRespondRequest(accept)) }) {
139:                 is RequestStatus.Success -> {
140:                     runCatching { refreshGroup(groupId) }
141:                     RequestStatus.Success(Unit)

143:                 is RequestStatus.Failed -> r

145:             }

148:     override suspend fun updateGroup(

150:         name: String?,

153:         // Backend PATCH schema is strict + name min(1): blank means "leave unchanged".
154:         val body = PatchGroupRequest(name?.trim()?.ifEmpty { null }, description)
155:         if (body.name == null && body.description == null) {

164:                 cacheMembers(r.data)
165:                 RequestStatus.Success(group)
166:             }
167:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
168:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
169:         }
170:     }

172:     override suspend fun deleteGroup(groupId: GroupId): RequestStatus<Unit> = withContext(io) {
173:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.deleteGroup(groupId) }) {
174:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
175:                 groups.deleteById(groupId)
176:                 RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
177:             }
178:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r

181:     }
182: 
183:     /** Explicit refreshes for VM first-paint gates; bypass the background throttle. */
184:     override suspend fun refreshGroups(): Boolean =
185:         listRefresh.tryRun(force = true) { fetchGroups() }
186: 
187:     override suspend fun refreshGroup(groupId: GroupId): Boolean =
188:         itemRefresh.tryRun(groupId, force = true) { fetchGroup(groupId) }
189: 
190:     private suspend fun fetchGroups(): Boolean {
191:         if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) return true
192:         return when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.getGroups() }) {
193:             // Signed out while in flight (signOut clears tables): never repopulate after the wipe.
194:             is RequestStatus.Success -> if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) {
195:                 true
196:             } else {
197:                 listError.value = null
198:                 r.data.forEach {
199:                     groups.upsert(it.toDomain().toEntity())
200:                     cacheMembers(it)
201:                 }
202:                 true
203:             }
204:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> {
205:                 listError.value = r
206:                 false
207:             }
208:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> true
209:         }
210:     }
211: 
212:     private suspend fun fetchGroup(groupId: GroupId): Boolean {
213:         if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) return true
214:         return when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.getGroup(groupId) }) {
215:             // See refreshGroups: no cache writes after sign-out.
216:             is RequestStatus.Success -> if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) {
217:                 true
218:             } else {
219:                 itemErrors.update { it - groupId }
220:                 groups.upsert(r.data.toDomain().toEntity())
221:                 cacheMembers(r.data)
222:                 true
223:             }
224:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> {
225:                 itemErrors.update { it + (groupId to r) }
226:                 false
227:             }
228:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> true
229:         }
230:     }
231: 
232:     /** Backend invites by email; domain contact carries the email (see RemoteMappers). */
233:     private suspend fun emailOf(userId: UserId): String? =
234:         users.observeById(userId).first()?.contact?.takeIf { it.contains("@") }
235:             ?: userId.takeIf { it.contains("@") }
236: 
237:     /** Wire members → Room users so displayName() resolves without a user lookup. */
238:     private suspend fun cacheMembers(dto: GroupDto) {
239:         dto.members.forEach {
240:             if (it.name.isBlank() && it.email.isBlank()) return@forEach
241:             users.upsert(mergeUser(users.observeById(it.id).first()?.toDomain(), it).toEntity())
242:         }
243:     }
244: 
245:     /** Merge wire member over cached row; never clobber a good cached name/contact with blank. */
246:     private fun mergeUser(existing: User?, wire: MemberDto): User {
247:         if (existing == null) return User(id = wire.id, name = wire.name, contact = wire.email)
248:         return existing.copy(
249:             name = if (wire.name.isNotBlank()) wire.name else existing.name,
250:             contact = if (wire.email.isNotBlank()) wire.email else existing.contact,
251:         )
252:     }
253: }

18: /**
19:  * In-memory GroupRepository. Writes apply to local state immediately (offline-first,
20:  * so previously retrieved data stays visible); sync is simulated via [sync] and
21:  * retried in the background through [queue] with eventual-success notification.
22:  */
23: class FakeGroupRepository(
24:     scope: CoroutineScope,
25:     private val notifications: NotificationService,
26:     private val queue: RetryQueue = RetryQueue(scope, notifications::notifyRetrySuccess),
27:     private val sync: SyncSimulator = SyncSimulator(),
28:     initial: List<Group> = emptyList(),
29: ) : GroupRepository {
30: 
31:     private val groups = MutableStateFlow(initial.associateBy(Group::id))
32: 
33:     /** QA hook: pending background syncs; drives offline/retry UI. */
34:     val pendingSync = queue.pendingCount
35: 
36:     fun setOnline(online: Boolean) = queue.setOnline(online)
37: 
38:     // In-memory: no network; explicit refreshes are no-ops (cache is already the truth).
39:     override suspend fun refreshGroups(): Boolean = true
40: 
41:     override suspend fun refreshGroup(groupId: GroupId): Boolean = true
42: 
43:     override fun observeGroups(): Flow<RequestStatus<List<Group>>> =
44:         groups.map { RequestStatus.Success(it.values.sortedBy(Group::name)) as RequestStatus<List<Group>> }
45:             .onStart { emit(RequestStatus.Pending) }
46: 
47:     override fun observeGroup(groupId: GroupId): Flow<RequestStatus<Group?>> =
48:         groups.map { RequestStatus.Success(it[groupId]) as RequestStatus<Group?> }
49:             .onStart { emit(RequestStatus.Pending) }
50: 
51:     override suspend fun createGroup(
52:         name: String,
53:         description: String,
54:         ownerId: UserId,
55:         memberIds: List<UserId>,
56:     ): RequestStatus<Group> {
57:         if (name.isBlank()) return RequestStatus.Failed("Group name must not be blank")
58:         val group = Group(
59:             id = UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
60:             name = name.trim(),
61:             description = description,
62:             ownerId = ownerId,
63:             memberIds = (memberIds + ownerId).distinct(),
64:         )
65:         groups.update { it + (group.id to group) }
66:         queue.enqueue("create-group:${group.id}") { sync.sync() }
67:         return RequestStatus.Success(group)
68:     }
69: 
70:     override suspend fun addMember(groupId: GroupId, userId: UserId): RequestStatus<Unit> =
71:         mutate(groupId, "add-member:$groupId:$userId") {
72:             it.copy(
73:                 memberIds = (it.memberIds + userId).distinct(),
74:                 invites = it.invites + (userId to InviteStatus.ACCEPTED),
75:             )
76:         }
77: 
78:     override suspend fun removeMember(groupId: GroupId, userId: UserId): RequestStatus<Unit> =
79:         mutate(groupId, "remove-member:$groupId:$userId") {
80:             it.copy(memberIds = it.memberIds - userId)
81:         }
82: 
83:     override suspend fun respondToInvite(groupId: GroupId, userId: UserId, accept: Boolean): RequestStatus<Unit> =
84:         mutate(groupId, "invite:$groupId:$userId") {
85:             val status = if (accept) InviteStatus.ACCEPTED else InviteStatus.DECLINED
86:             it.copy(
87:                 invites = it.invites + (userId to status),
88:                 memberIds = if (accept) (it.memberIds + userId).distinct() else it.memberIds - userId,
89:             )
90:         }
91: 
92:     override suspend fun updateGroup(
93:         groupId: GroupId,
94:         name: String?,
95:         description: String?,
96:     ): RequestStatus<Group> {
97:         val current = groups.value[groupId] ?: return RequestStatus.Failed("Group not found")
98:         val updated = current.copy(
99:             name = name?.trim()?.ifEmpty { null } ?: current.name,
100:             description = description ?: current.description,
101:         )
102:         groups.update { it + (groupId to updated) }
103:         queue.enqueue("update-group:$groupId") { sync.sync() }
104:         return RequestStatus.Success(updated)
105:     }
106: 
107:     override suspend fun deleteGroup(groupId: GroupId): RequestStatus<Unit> {
108:         val removed = groups.value.containsKey(groupId)
109:         groups.update { it - groupId }
110:         queue.enqueue("delete-group:$groupId") { sync.sync() }
111:         return if (removed) RequestStatus.Success(Unit) else RequestStatus.Failed("Group not found")
112:     }
113: 
114:     private fun mutate(groupId: GroupId, label: String, transform: (Group) -> Group): RequestStatus<Unit> {
115:         val current = groups.value[groupId] ?: return RequestStatus.Failed("Group not found")
116:         groups.update { it + (groupId to transform(current)) }
117:         queue.enqueue(label) { sync.sync() }
118:         return RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
119:     }
120: }

45: /**
46:  * Offline-first ExpenseRepository: Room cache drives reads; balances/settlements
47:  * derive via [DebtCalculator] like the fakes. Writes go to the network first then the
48:  * cache. [recordSettlement] POSTs to `/payments` (server truth) and mirrors the
49:  * repayment locally with the same id the server returns as txId, so the next
50:  * expenses refresh (which now folds payments in with `kind: "settlement"`) upserts
51:  * over the mirror instead of duplicating it. No Hilt binding yet — arch owner wires it.
52:  *
53:  * Fault tolerance: retryable failures ([addExpense]/[recordSettlement]) write a
54:  * client-id Room mirror immediately (stays visible offline) and park the op in
55:  * [Outbox] (WorkManager, `CONNECTED` + exponential backoff); the suspend call
56:  * still returns [RequestStatus.Failed] at once so the UI never blocks.
57:  * Payment mirrors reuse the existing row for the same settlement identity, and
58:  * the stable `payment-<group>-<from>-<to>-<amount>` op key keeps double-tap /
59:  * retry from minting duplicate charges.
60:  *
61:  * Reconciliation: every successful per-group refresh prunes client-mirror rows
62:  * ([ExpenseEntity.isLocalMirror]) absent from the server snapshot, so a
63:  * dropped op (past `OUTBOX_MAX_ATTEMPTS`) stops diverging balances. Server
64:  * rows are never pruned.
65:  */
66: @Singleton
67: class ApiExpenseRepository @Inject constructor(
68:     private val api: ApiService,
69:     db: EasyTrackerDatabase,
70:     private val prefs: UserPreferences,
71:     @IoDispatcher private val io: CoroutineDispatcher,
72:     private val outbox: Outbox,
73: ) : ExpenseRepository {
74:     private val expenses = db.expenseDao()
75:     private val cachedGroups = db.groupDao()
76:     private val clear401: suspend () -> Unit = { prefs.clearAuth() }
77:     // Singleton-lived: background refreshes never block collectors (cache emits first).
78:     private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + io)
79:     private val itemRefresh = KeyedRefreshSingleFlight()
80:     private val allRefresh = RefreshSingleFlight()
81:     // Last network failure per pass; an empty cache + a recorded failure emits Failed
82:     // (a warm cache always wins, so offline reads stay visible).
83:     private val itemErrors = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, RequestStatus.Failed>>(emptyMap())
84:     private val allError = MutableStateFlow<RequestStatus.Failed?>(null)
85: 
86:     // Cache-first: Room emits immediately, per-group refresh runs in the background
87:     // single-flighted (observeBalances/Settlements re-collect this flow — one shared pass).
88:     // Empty cache + failed refresh surfaces Failed (retryable) instead of fake empty Success.
89:     override fun observeExpenses(groupId: GroupId): Flow<RequestStatus<List<Expense>>> =
90:         combine(expenses.observeByGroup(groupId), itemErrors) { rows, errs ->
91:             val list = rows.map { it.toDomain() }
92:             val err = errs[groupId]
93:             if (list.isEmpty() && err != null) err else RequestStatus.Success(list)
94:         }.onStart {
95:             itemRefresh.launchIn(scope, groupId) { fetchExpenses(groupId) }
96:         }.flowOn(io)
97: 
98:     override fun observeBalances(groupId: GroupId): Flow<RequestStatus<List<Balance>>> =
99:         observeExpenses(groupId).map { status ->
100:             when (status) {
101:                 is RequestStatus.Success -> RequestStatus.Success(
102:                     DebtCalculator.calculateBalances(status.data)
103:                         .map { (id, net) -> Balance(id, net) }
104:                         .sortedByDescending { it.netCents },
105:                 )
106:                 is RequestStatus.Pending -> status
107:                 is RequestStatus.Failed -> status
108:             }
109:         }
110: 
111:     override fun observeSettlements(groupId: GroupId): Flow<RequestStatus<List<Settlement>>> =
112:         observeExpenses(groupId).map { status ->
113:             when (status) {
114:                 is RequestStatus.Success -> RequestStatus.Success(
115:                     DebtCalculator.minimizeSettlements(DebtCalculator.calculateBalances(status.data)),
116:                 )
117:                 is RequestStatus.Pending -> status
118:                 is RequestStatus.Failed -> status
119:             }
120:         }
121: 
122:     override suspend fun addExpense(expense: Expense): RequestStatus<Expense> = withContext(io) {
123:         if (expense.amountCents < 0L) return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("Amount must be >= 0")
124:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) {
125:             api.createExpense(
126:                 expense.groupId,
127:                 CreateExpenseRequest(
128:                     payerId = expense.payerId,
129:                     amountCents = expense.amountCents,
130:                     description = expense.description,
131:                     splitAmong = expense.splitAmong,
132:                     receiptUri = expense.receiptUri,
133:                 ),
134:             )
135:         }) {
136:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
137:                 val stored = r.data.toDomain()
138:                 expenses.upsert(stored.toEntity())
139:                 RequestStatus.Success(stored)
140:             }
141:             // Retryable (e.g. temp network loss): mirror locally so the row stays
142:             // visible offline, park the op in the Outbox, fail fast (non-blocking).
143:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> if (r.retryable) {
144:                 val mirror = expense.copy(
145:                     id = UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
146:                     createdAt = expense.createdAt.takeIf { it != 0L } ?: System.currentTimeMillis(),
147:                 )
148:                 expenses.upsert(mirror.toEntity(isLocalMirror = true))
149:                 outbox.enqueueExpense(mirror)
150:                 r
151:             } else {
152:                 r
153:             }
154:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
155:         }
156:     }
157: 
158:     // Cache-only by design: no global history route exists, so collecting must never fan out
159:     // N per-group calls (Account chips derive from this cache). History refreshes explicitly.
160:     // Empty cache + failed explicit refresh surfaces Failed instead of fake empty Success.
161:     override fun observeAllExpenses(): Flow<RequestStatus<List<Expense>>> =
162:         combine(expenses.observeAll(), allError) { rows, err ->
163:             val list = rows.map { it.toDomain() }
164:             if (list.isEmpty() && err != null) err else RequestStatus.Success(list)
165:         }.flowOn(io)
166: 
167:     override suspend fun updateExpense(
168:         expenseId: ExpenseId,
169:         amountCents: Long?,
170:         description: String?,
171:         splitAmong: List<String>?,
172:         receiptUri: String?,
173:     ): RequestStatus<Expense> = withContext(io) {
174:         if (amountCents != null && amountCents < 0L) return@withContext RequestStatus.Failed("Amount must be >= 0")
175:         val body = PatchExpenseRequest(amountCents, description, splitAmong, receiptUri)
176:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.patchExpense(expenseId, body) }) {
177:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
178:                 val stored = r.data.toDomain()
179:                 expenses.upsert(stored.toEntity())
180:                 RequestStatus.Success(stored)
181:             }
182:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
183:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
184:         }
185:     }
186: 
187:     override suspend fun deleteExpense(expenseId: ExpenseId): RequestStatus<Unit> = withContext(io) {
188:         when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.deleteExpense(expenseId) }) {
189:             is RequestStatus.Success -> {
190:                 expenses.deleteById(expenseId)
191:                 RequestStatus.Success(Unit)
192:             }
193:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> r
194:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
195:         }
196:     }
197: 
198:     override suspend fun recordSettlement(groupId: GroupId, settlement: Settlement): RequestStatus<Expense> =
199:         withContext(io) {
200:             // Backend derives the payer (from) from the JWT; toId/amount are the body.
201:             when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) {
202:                 api.recordPayment(groupId, PaymentRequest(settlement.toId, settlement.amountCents))
203:             }) {
204:                 is RequestStatus.Success -> {
205:                     val mirror = paymentMirror(r.data.txId, groupId, settlement)
206:                     expenses.upsert(mirror.toEntity())
207:                     RequestStatus.Success(mirror)
208:                 }
209:                 // Retryable: reuse the existing client mirror for this settlement
210:                 // identity when present (upsert over it, same stable op key), so
211:                 // retry/double-tap never stacks duplicate mirror rows or charges.
212:                 is RequestStatus.Failed -> if (r.retryable) {
213:                     val reusedId = findReusablePaymentMirror(expenses.localMirrors(groupId), settlement)?.id
214:                     val mirror = Expense(
215:                         id = reusedId ?: UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
216:                         groupId = groupId,
217:                         payerId = settlement.fromId,
218:                         amountCents = settlement.amountCents,
219:                         description = "Settlement",
220:                         splitAmong = listOf(settlement.toId),
221:                         createdAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
222:                         isSettlement = true,
223:                     )
224:                     expenses.upsert(mirror.toEntity(isLocalMirror = true))
225:                     outbox.enqueuePayment(groupId, settlement, mirror)
226:                     r
227:                 } else {
228:                     r
229:                 }
230:                 is RequestStatus.Pending -> r
231:             }
232:         }
233: 
234:     /** Explicit per-group refresh for the Detail first-paint gate; bypasses the throttle. */
235:     override suspend fun refreshExpenses(groupId: GroupId): Boolean =
236:         itemRefresh.tryRun(groupId, force = true) { fetchExpenses(groupId) }
237: 
238:     private suspend fun fetchExpenses(groupId: GroupId): Boolean {
239:         if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) return true
240:         return when (val r = safeApiCall(clear401) { api.getExpenses(groupId) }) {
241:             // Signed out while in flight (signOut clears tables): never repopulate after the wipe.
242:             is RequestStatus.Success -> if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) {
243:                 true
244:             } else {
245:                 itemErrors.update { it - groupId }
246:                 r.data.forEach { expenses.upsert(it.toDomain().toEntity()) }
247:                 // Dropped-op reconciliation: prune client mirrors the server never
248:                 // saw (op dropped past OUTBOX_MAX_ATTEMPTS); the worker re-adds a
249:                 // still-pending op's row on success, so pruning is self-healing.
250:                 val serverIds = r.data.map { it.id }.toSet()
251:                 staleLocalMirrorIds(expenses.localMirrors(groupId), serverIds)
252:                     .forEach { expenses.deleteById(it) }
253:                 true
254:             }
255:             is RequestStatus.Failed -> {
256:                 itemErrors.update { it + (groupId to r) }
257:                 false
258:             }
259:             is RequestStatus.Pending -> true
260:         }
261:     }
262: 
263:     /**
264:      * Explicit cross-group refresh: single-flighted, max [MAX_PARALLEL_GROUP_FETCH]
265:      * concurrent per-group fetches. Each group fetch shares the item gate with
266:      * Detail/background passes, so Groups + History + Account never open duplicate
267:      * sockets for the same group.
268:      */
269:     override suspend fun refreshAllExpenses(): Boolean = allRefresh.tryRun(force = true) {
270:         if (prefs.authToken.first().isNullOrBlank()) return@tryRun true
271:         val ids = cachedGroups.observeAll().first().map { it.id }
272:         val gate = Semaphore(MAX_PARALLEL_GROUP_FETCH)
273:         val results = coroutineScope {
274:             ids.map { id ->
275:                 async { gate.withPermit { itemRefresh.tryRun(id, block = { fetchExpenses(id) }) } }
276:             }.awaitAll()
277:         }
278:         val ok = results.all { it }
279:         allError.value = if (ok) null else itemErrors.value.values.firstOrNull()
280:         ok
281:     }
282: 
283:     private companion object {
284:         const val MAX_PARALLEL_GROUP_FETCH = 4
285:     }
286: }
287: 
288: /**
289:  * Existing client mirror for the same settlement identity (same payer, recipient
290:  * and amount, settlement-shaped), if any. Compared on domain shape so the
291:  * storage encoding stays an impl detail.
292:  */
293: internal fun findReusablePaymentMirror(
294:     mirrors: List<ExpenseEntity>,
295:     settlement: Settlement,
296: ): ExpenseEntity? = mirrors.filter { it.isSettlement }.firstOrNull {
297:     val e = it.toDomain()
298:     e.payerId == settlement.fromId &&
299:         e.amountCents == settlement.amountCents &&
300:         e.description == "Settlement" &&
301:         e.splitAmong == listOf(settlement.toId)
302: }
303: 
304: /** Client-mirror ids missing from the latest server snapshot — safe to prune. */
305: internal fun staleLocalMirrorIds(
306:     mirrors: List<ExpenseEntity>,
307:     serverIds: Set<String>,
308: ): List<String> = mirrors.filter { it.id !in serverIds }.map { it.id }

154: groupsRouter.get("/groups/:id/summary", async (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
155:   const gid = req.params.id;
156:   if (!await needMember(gid, req.user!.id)) return res.status(404).json({ error: "not found" });
157:   const members = (await q("SELECT user_id FROM group_members WHERE group_id=$1 AND status='ACCEPTED'", [gid])).rows.map((x) => x.user_id as string);
158:   const expenses = (await q("SELECT payer_id,amount_cents,split_among FROM expenses WHERE group_id=$1", [gid])).rows;
159:   const payments = (await q("SELECT from_id,to_id,amount_cents FROM payments WHERE group_id=$1", [gid])).rows;
160:   const net: Record<string, number> = Object.fromEntries(members.map((m) => [m, 0]));
161:   let totalCents = 0;
162:   for (const e of expenses) {
163:     const amt = Number(e.amount_cents);
164:     totalCents += amt;
165:     const split: string[] = e.split_among ?? [];
166:     if (!split.length) continue;
167:     net[e.payer_id] ??= 0;
168:     for (const id of split) net[id] ??= 0;
169:     const base = Math.floor(amt / split.length);
170:     const rem = amt % split.length;
171:     net[e.payer_id] += amt;
172:     split.forEach((id, i) => { net[id] -= base + (i < rem ? 1 : 0); });
173:   }
174:   for (const pm of payments) {
175:     const amt = Number(pm.amount_cents);
176:     net[pm.from_id] ??= 0;
177:     net[pm.to_id] ??= 0;
178:     net[pm.from_id] += amt;
179:     net[pm.to_id] -= amt;
180:   }
181:   const ids = Object.keys(net);
182:   const debtors = ids.filter((m) => net[m] < 0).sort((a, b) => net[a] - net[b]);
183:   const creditors = ids.filter((m) => net[m] > 0).sort((a, b) => net[b] - net[a]);
184:   const settlements: { fromId: string; toId: string; amountCents: number }[] = [];
185:   let i = 0, j = 0;
186:   const d = debtors.map((m) => ({ id: m, amt: -net[m] }));
187:   const c = creditors.map((m) => ({ id: m, amt: net[m] }));
188:   while (i < d.length && j < c.length) {
189:     const amt = Math.min(d[i].amt, c[j].amt);
190:     settlements.push({ fromId: d[i].id, toId: c[j].id, amountCents: amt });
191:     d[i].amt -= amt; c[j].amt -= amt;
192:     if (d[i].amt === 0) i++;
193:     if (c[j].amt === 0) j++;
194:   }
195:   res.json({ balances: net, settlements, totalCents });
196: });

26: /**
27:  * Edit profile. Saving runs in the VM owner ([onSave] persists name/contact onto the
28:  * current profile); [saveResult] success fires [onDone] (the graph confirms with a
29:  * Snackbar). Phone is local-only — the User model has no phone field.
30:  *
31:  * Pending disables the form and shows progress; failed shows the repo message with
32:  * Retry (re-submits the last values) and Dismiss ([onDismissError] clears the VM
33:  * result). Cancel stays enabled throughout — failure never traps the user.

36: fun EditProfileScreen(

43:     saveResult: RequestStatus<Unit>? = null,

95: /** Plain-text euro rendering for sharesheets (mirrors AmountText without a composable). */
96: fun formatCentsPlain(amountCents: Long): String {
97:     val sign = if (amountCents < 0) "-" else ""
98:     return "$sign€${"%.2f".format(kotlin.math.abs(amountCents) / 100.0)}"
99: }
100: 
101: /** Shareable receipt body from real payment props — no placeholder amounts. */
102: fun receiptText(amountCents: Long, group: String, txId: String): String = buildString {
103:     appendLine("Payment receipt")
104:     appendLine("Amount: ${formatCentsPlain(amountCents)}")
105:     appendLine("Group: $group")
106:     if (txId.isNotBlank()) appendLine("Transaction: $txId")
107: }.trimEnd()
108: 
109: /**
110:  * Group display label: the resolved name, else a short-id fallback — never a raw
111:  * UUID as the primary line. Null when neither is known (callers omit the line).
112:  */
113: internal fun displayGroupLabel(groupId: String, groupName: String?): String? {
114:     if (!groupName.isNullOrBlank()) return groupName
115:     if (groupId.isBlank()) return null
116:     return if (groupId.length > 8) "Group ••••${groupId.takeLast(4)}" else groupId
117: }

31:     try {

  "prompt": "Create a thorough, simple, readable MVVM-C architecture diagram for EasyTracker as ./architecture.drawio (repo root D:\\GitHub\\EasyTracker, draw.io uncompressed XML format that opens in diagrams.net / drawio desktop).\n\nStack: Native Android + Kotlin 2.2.10, AGP 9.5.1, compileSdk/targetSdk 37, minSdk 24, Compose BOM 2026.02.01, Material3, Hilt, Room, DataStore, Retrofit, single module :app, backend Express+TS (backend/src: routes/groups.ts, routes/expenses.ts, routes/auth.ts, middleware/auth.ts, db.ts, schema.sql via initSchema).\n\nRESEARCH FIRST (relevance-only, stop once conventions clear): identify the real layers from code — navigation/AppNavHost.kt + Routes.kt (Coordinator), ui/screens/* (View: GroupsOverview, GroupDetail, SetupDebt, MakePayment, PaymentHistory, Receipt, PaymentSuccess/Failed, Account, Settings, EditProfile, EditGroup, CreateGroup, Auth, Splash, ScreenScaffold), ui/viewmodels/* (ViewModel: Groups, GroupDetail, SetupDebt, MakePayment, PaymentHistory, Profile, Auth + core/BaseViewModel + RequestStatus + StatusBus), domain/* (Model: Group, Expense, Settlement, User + DebtCalculator, GroupList, GroupHistory, AccountStats), data/* (Repository interfaces Group/Expense/User/Notification + ApiGroup/ApiExpense/ApiUserRepository, fake/* for tests, local/ Room EasyTrackerDatabase/CacheDao/CacheEntities/Mappers, prefs/UserPreferences DataStore, remote/ ApiService/Dtos/RemoteMappers, outbox/, RefreshSingleFlight), di/AppModule+NetworkModule, MainActivity/EasyTrackerApp entry. Include backend column (auth/groups/expenses routes, Postgres) and cross-cutting (Hilt DI, RequestStatus/ApiResult/safeApiCall, StatusBus/Snackbar).\n\nDIAGRAM REQUIREMENTS:\n- One page (Page-1, ~1600x1000), left→right lanes: UI (Compose Screens + NavHost Coordinator) → ViewModel → Domain → Data (Repository→Remote/Local/Prefs) → Backend/DB. Clear MVVM-C labeling: C = AppNavHost/Routes as Coordinator mediating View↔ViewModel.\n- Boxes for every important file/class above (group small helpers: e.g. one box \"Screens (12)\" with listed names is NOT enough — show each screen as a small node, but keep tidy with swimlane containers + color coding per layer).\n- Arrows labeled with mechanism: observe (StateFlow<RequestStatus>), invoke (suspend), Retrofit HTTPS, Room Flow, DataStore prefs, Hilt @Inject.\n- Readability: orthogonal routing, no overlapping edges/labels, generous spacing, layer colors (e.g. blue UI, green VM, yellow domain, purple data, gray backend), legend box, title + date + \"MVVM-C\" note. Iterate 2-3 times: draft, then fix overlaps/crossings, then final polish.\n- Write valid drawio XML: <mxfile><diagram name=\"Architecture\"><mxGraphModel><root><mxCell id=\"0\"/><mxCell id=\"1\" parent=\"0\"/>... standard mxCell/mxGeometry with style=\"rounded=1;fillColor=...;strokeColor=...;fontColor=...\". Verify file parses (open tags balanced) and stays under ~200KB.\n\nDo NOT edit any other files. Report: path written, node/edge counts, how you verified readability + XML validity."
}

diff --git a/backend/src/routes/auth.ts b/backend/src/routes/auth.ts
index a3243b4..e6c6df4 100644
--- a/backend/src/routes/auth.ts
+++ b/backend/src/routes/auth.ts
@@ -38,3 +38,25 @@ authRouter.get("/me", requireAuth, async (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
   if (!r.rowCount) return res.status(404).json({ error: "not found" });
   res.json(toUserJson(r.rows[0]));
 });
+
+const patchMe = z.object({ name: z.string().min(1).optional(), contact: z.string().optional(), email: z.string().email().optional() });
+
+// Profile write: persists name/contact so the profile is visible to others and
+// survives relogin (server is the source of truth; GET /me returns it fresh).
+// Email changes are validated + unique-checked like registration.
+authRouter.patch("/me", requireAuth, async (req: AuthedRequest, res) => {
+  const p = patchMe.safeParse(req.body);
+  if (!p.success) return res.status(400).json({ error: "invalid body" });
+  const { name, contact, email } = p.data;
+  if (email !== undefined) {
+    const taken = await q("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email=$1 AND id<>$2", [email.toLowerCase(), req.user!.id]);
+    if (taken.rowCount) return res.status(409).json({ error: "email taken" });
+  }
+  const r = await q(
+    `UPDATE users SET name=COALESCE($1,name), contact=COALESCE($2,contact), email=COALESCE($3,email)
+     WHERE id=$4 RETURNING id,name,email,contact`,
+    [name?.trim() ?? null, contact ?? null, email?.toLowerCase() ?? null, req.user!.id]
+  );
+  if (!r.rowCount) return res.status(404).json({ error: "not found" });
+  res.json(toUserJson(r.rows[0]));
+});

## Assistant

_Thinking:_



All done and verified. Final report:

## Debt permissions — any member can create, nobody creates "for" another
- **Backend** (`backend/src/routes/groups.ts`): POST `/groups/:id/expenses` forces payer = caller; a `payerId` that isn't yours → `403 payer must be yourself`. (`expenses.ts`: PATCH restricted to payer-or-owner; **DELETE got the same gate in review** — see risk note.)
- **Android**: `GroupDetailScreen` — "Set up a debt" moved out of the owner gate (every member sees it; "Edit group" stays owner-only). `SetupDebtScreen` — the "Paid by" dropdown is gone, replaced by a display-only `Paid by: <you>` line; `AppNavHost` always saves with the session user id, null until session loads (Save stays disabled, never another member).
- **Tests**: `testDebugUnitTest` green, incl. new `ContractMirrorTest` (blank-payer guards + documented fake divergence).

## Unknown emails can't join groups
- **Backend**: POST `/groups` pre-validates all `memberEmails` inside a transaction — first unknown email → `404 user not found: <email>`, nothing created (no orphaned group). POST `/groups/:id/members` 404 kept.
- **Android** (`ApiGroupRepository`): the fallback-refresh that used to mask 404s as success now only applies to retryable/network failures; "user not found" surfaces verbatim in Create/Edit group.

## Profile actually saves
- **Backend**: new `PATCH /me` (name/contact/email, 409 on taken email); GET `/me` returns it fresh.
- **Android** (`ApiUserRepository`): `saveCurrentProfile` is network-first (PATCH → DataStore + Room), offline failures update the local mirror but return `Failed` instead of silently succeeding; `refreshMe` is server-wins. Name changes now persist across relogin and are visible to other users (member lists render the server `name`).
- Note: the EditProfile "Email" field is relabeled **Contact** — it edits `contact`, never the login email (wiring the real login-email change would silently reassign identity, so it was deliberately left out).

## Icons (20/20 from `Icons.zip` → `res/drawable/`)
Wired per your mapping: `arrow_back` back button, `add` create-group/setup-debt, `group/person/settings` bottom tabs, `person_add/person_remove` invite/remove, `payments` make-payment, `credit_card` paying-as, `receipt` history rows, `download` receipt share, `edit` profile, `logout` sign-outs, `notifications(_active)` settings rows, `check_circle/cancel` success/failed (replacing the hand-drawn canvas), `error/schedule` error/pending states. Two deviations: no search field exists in the app (nothing to wire `search` to — asset is in place), and there's no standalone remind button (`notifications_active` went on the Reminders row).

## Typography
`Type.kt`: explicit `FontFamily.SansSerif`, body text ≥16sp/24sp (`bodyMedium` 14→16, `labelMedium` kept captions-only at 14/20 with a KDoc rule), light = near-black on near-white / dark = inverse (documented against ISO 9241-303 §§5.5.2–5.5.4 + 9241-125). Grep-confirmed: no serif/Caveat remnants.

## Architecture diagram
`./architecture.drawio` (29KB, valid XML): one page, 5 lanes (UI+Coordinator → ViewModel → Domain → Data → Backend/Postgres), all 16 screens, 7 VMs, repos, Room/DataStore/Retrofit, Hilt + status cross-cutting, 12 labeled edges + legend.

## Verification
- `:app:testDebugUnitTest` — green, 0 failures/errors (my own run, post-fixer tree)
- `npx tsc --noEmit` — clean
- Reviewer gate: no blockers; all 8 actionable findings applied by fixer, re-verified

## Open risks / decisions for you
1. **Expense DELETE is now payer-or-owner only** (was: any member). Consistent with edit, but it's a behavior change — say the word if you want any-member delete back.
2. Login-email change is intentionally unsupported (Contact label); backend already supports `PATCH /me {email}` if you later want a real "change email" flow with re-login.
3. I haven't decided when you should commit — that's yours to call.

## Assistant

Status across the three issues:

- **Groups refresh failure + stale Refresh button** — with the backend/data owner now (root-cause + real error messages + expense refresh included in the pass).
- **Closed-app notifications** — two owners in parallel: backend/data builds the real notification poster + 15-minute sync worker (diffing, de-dupe, honoring your Settings toggles, scheduled on login / cancelled on logout); UI wires tap-to-screen deep links (debt → group, payment → receipt/history), the Android 13+ permission ask, and safe cold-start routing.

One expectation to set honestly: Android itself forbids periodic background work faster than every 15 minutes — that's an OS floor, not something code can beat. So worst case a notification can lag ~15 minutes behind the event. Every app open also syncs instantly, which covers most real use. If you later need true instant delivery (seconds), that requires Firebase push — the deep-link and channel plumbing being built now carries straight over.
