## Assistant

Everything's green on the final tree. Full report:

## 1. "Couldn't refresh groups" — fixed, and the error now tells the truth
- **Root cause**: the request path itself was healthy — the app was *discarding the real error* and always showing the generic string. With a warm cache, failures were invisible in the data flow and the Boolean refresh contract dropped the reason.
- **Fix**: `GroupRepository.refresh*()` now returns `RequestStatus<Unit>` end-to-end (interface, API + fake impls, single-flight, both ViewModels), so the screen shows the actual cause — connection failure, timeout, HTTP error — with "Couldn't refresh groups." only as a last-resort fallback.
- **If it still fails for you, read the new message**: prime suspect is the base URL — the default `http://10.0.2.2:3000` only works on an emulator. On a physical device (or if the backend isn't running) you'll now see the connect failure spelled out. Point the app at your machine's LAN IP in that case.

## 2. Refresh button now actually refreshes
- The pass was groups-only while the € nets derive from cached expenses — that's why you had to navigate away and back. `launchRefresh` now runs **groups + expenses together** and only clears the error when both succeed; the message names which pass failed. The 1-second debounce and loading gates are unchanged.

## 3. Real closed-app notifications, tap → correct screen
- **Engine**: new `SystemNotificationService` (real `NotificationManager`, 3 channels — debts and payments are high-importance so they peek on lockscreen) replaces the fake that recorded-but-never-posted; new `SyncWorker` runs every 15 min on connectivity, diffs server vs. last-seen, and notifies: new debts, settlements where you're the recipient (never your own actions, bursts collapse into one summary), pending invites. First run seeds silently so you don't get spammed with history. Honors your Settings toggles per kind.
- **Scheduling**: starts on login/register, cancelled on logout. Plus every app open syncs instantly.
- **Tap routing**: `easytracker://` deep links — new debt → its group, payment update → history, reminder → groups; cold start, warm tap, logged-out tap (→ Login, never a crash), and malformed links (→ normal start) all handled. Back behaves correctly.
- **Permission**: one-time, dismissible prompt on first Groups screen after login (never nags; denial directs to system settings from the Settings screen). Needs the Android 13+ runtime grant to show anything — that prompt covers it.
- **Review pass applied**: fixed double-navigation on tap (NavHost already handles intents — the manual handler doubled every stack), snapshot replace-instead-of-grow, persist-before-notify, blank-ID validation, permission-card flash.

## Verification
- `:app:testDebugUnitTest` + `:app:assembleDebug` — **both pass, 0 failures/errors**, including new `SyncPlannerTest` (11), `NotificationDeepLinkTest` (6), `ContractMirrorTest` (3). Independent reviewer pass: no blockers remaining.

## Honest limits
- **15 minutes is an OS-enforced floor** for periodic background work — worst case a notification lags ~15 min behind the event. True instant (seconds) needs Firebase push later; all the channel/deep-link plumbing carries over.
- Device-tap QA (real notification shade → tap → lands correctly, cold and warm) still belongs on a physical device — worth one manual pass.

## Assistant

What's happening, in plain terms: the app waits for **both** the groups and the expenses request to finish before it stops showing the spinner or shows any error. If one of those requests hangs in limbo (the server accepts it but never answers — which this backend does when its database is unreachable) instead of cleanly failing, the spinner waits forever, no error is ever set, and the Refresh button stays disabled so you can't retry out of it.

The fix now in progress:
- A hard timeout on network calls, so a hanging request becomes a proper error after ~30 seconds instead of never.
- The loading gate only waits for the groups request; an expenses failure shows as an error message next to loaded content.
- The error screen always wins over an endless spinner, and Refresh/Retry stay enabled when there's an error.

One thing worth checking on your side while it builds: if your backend isn't running (or you're on a physical device still pointed at the emulator-only address), the app will now fail fast with the real reason spelled out instead of spinning. I'll confirm tests once the fix lands.

## Assistant

Fixed. Your infinite spinner is gone — here's what changed and what you'll see now:

- **A hanging request can no longer hang the app.** Network calls now time out (~30s) and each refresh pass is individually time-bounded, so the worst case is a 30-second wait, never forever.
- **The loading gate only waits for groups.** Expenses load independently — if they fail, you get your list plus a small "Couldn't refresh expenses." note instead of a spinner.
- **Errors always beat the spinner.** If something fails, you get the error screen with a working Retry, and the Refresh button stays enabled whenever an error is present — no more dead ends.
- Same treatment inside group details (its history section had the identical trap).

**What you'll see now, worst case** (empty cache + dead backend): ~30s of honest loading, then an error screen naming the cause with Retry — same for the detail screen. Reinstall/restart to pick this up, and if the message names a connection failure, it's the backend address or a stopped backend (same prime suspect as before).

Verification: `:app:testDebugUnitTest` + `:app:assembleDebug` both green, including a new `RefreshTimeoutTest` pinning the timeout/gate behavior, plus the pre-existing gate test confirming failures surface instead of spinning.
